## E. Standing Scientist Rules

1. Execute S-104 for every new reviewer batch: read, judge independently, accept valuable points, and record dissent / redundancy.
2. Do not trigger a full review unless the PDF, evidence state, or presentation state has materially changed.
3. Do not turn local open-weight diagnostics into paper claims until same-backbone evidence is positive and stable.
4. Keep new documents and TODO updates in English.
5. Keep source-of-truth analyses under `docs/scientist/analysis/`, handoffs under `docs/scientist/handoffs/`, and generated outputs under `artifacts/`.
6. Every user-facing status report must state the next concrete action the scientist will take; do not only summarize completed work.
